# themes
if $theme['name'] = '':
	$theme['name'] = 'Dynamic 默认'
	$theme['type'] = 'dynamic'
	gs 'themes', 'set_theme', '动态默认', 'dynamic'
end


	!!====================================================================!!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!                           THEME MENU                               !!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!====================================================================!!


!! Toggle dynamic ↔ static, fix up $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'], reapply, then return.
!! Called via exec link; caller does `& gt 'themes', 'menu'` after.
if $ARGS[0] = 'menu_toggle':
	if cfg_vars['themetype'] = 0:
		!! Switching to Static
		cfg_vars['themetype'] = 1
		if $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '默认' or $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '':
			$cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '白色'
		end
		gs 'themes', 'set_theme', $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'], 'static'
	else
		!! Switching to Dynamic
		cfg_vars['themetype'] = 0
		if $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '白色' or $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '黑色' or $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '现代灰' or $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '自定义' or $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '':
			$cfg_vars['theme_main_name'] = '默认'
		end
		gs 'themes', 'set_theme', 'Dynamic ' + $cfg_vars['theme_main_name'], 'dynamic'
	end
	gs '$menu_obnovit'
	exit
end


!! Render one theme preview <td> into the current table row.
!! ARGS[1] = base name (no 'Dynamic ' prefix), ARGS[2] = type ('dynamic'|'static')
!! Reads $themes_menu_cur (full name of currently active theme) from parent scope.
if $ARGS[0] = 'menu_cell':
	$temp_mc_full = $iif($ARGS[2] = 'dynamic', 'Dynamic ' + $ARGS[1], $ARGS[1])

	!! Temporarily load this theme''s indoors palette to read its colours
	$theme['name'] = $temp_mc_full
	$theme['type'] = $ARGS[2]
	gs 'themes', 'get_theme', 'indoors'

	$temp_mc_bg  = func('shortgs', 'rgb_to_hex', theme['bcolor'])
	$temp_mc_fg  = func('shortgs', 'rgb_to_hex', theme['fcolor'])

	!! Active theme: bold outline using its accent colour
	$temp_mc_sel = $iif($themes_menu_cur = $temp_mc_full, 'outline:3px solid ' + $theme_hex['accent'] + '; outline-offset:-3px; ', '')

	!! Entire cell is the link (display:block fills the padded area)
	!! Fixed width for uniform sizing; height driven naturally by padding
	*p '<td bgcolor="' + $temp_mc_bg + '" width="250" align="center" style="background:' + $temp_mc_bg + '; ' + $temp_mc_sel + 'width:250px; text-align:center; white-space:nowrap; border-radius:4px; padding:0; overflow:hidden;"><a href="exec: $cfg_vars[''theme_main_name''] = ''<<$ARGS[1]>>'' & gs ''themes'', ''set_theme'', ''<<$temp_mc_full>>'', ''<<$ARGS[2]>>'' & gs ''$menu_obnovit'' & gt ''themes'', ''menu''" style="display:block; padding:20px 28px; color:' + $temp_mc_fg + '; text-decoration:none; font-weight:' + $iif($themes_menu_cur = $temp_mc_full, 'bold', 'normal') + ';"><<$ARGS[1]>></a></td>'

	killvar 'temp_mc_full'
	killvar 'temp_mc_bg'
	killvar 'temp_mc_fg'
	killvar 'temp_mc_sel'
	exit
end


!! Main theme picker page.
if $ARGS[0] = 'menu':
	*clr & cla

	!! Remember where we came from so the Leave act can return
	if $themes_menu_ret_loc = '':
		$themes_menu_ret_loc = $loc
		$themes_menu_ret_arg = $loc_arg
	end
	!! settingmode: set once on first entry (tracked by separate flag)
	if themes_menu_active = 0:
		themes_menu_active = 1
		themes_menu_prev_settingmode = settingmode
		settingmode = 1
	end

	!! Snapshot current theme name/mode
	$themes_menu_cur  = $theme['name']
	$themes_menu_mode = $iif(cfg_vars['themetype'] = 0, 'dynamic', 'static')

	!! Restore current theme NOW so is_dark and theme_hex are available for the whole page
	$theme['name'] = $themes_menu_cur
	$theme['type'] = $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'get_theme', 'indoors'
	bcolor = theme['bcolor']
	fcolor = theme['fcolor']
	lcolor = theme['lcolor']
	fsize  = theme['fsize']
	$fname = $theme['fname']

	!! Chrome colours
	$tm_label_fg = $iif(theme['is_dark'] = 1, '#cccccc', '#333333')
	$tm_cell_bg  = $iif(theme['is_dark'] = 1, '#000000', '#ffffff')
	$tm_fg_hex   = func('shortgs', 'rgb_to_hex', fcolor)

	!! ── Dynamic / Static toggle ──────────────────────────────────────────
	*p '<center>'
	if cfg_vars['themetype'] = 0:
		*p '<a href="exec: gs ''themes'', ''menu_toggle'' & gt ''themes'', ''menu''" style="display:inline-block; font-size:1.15em; font-weight:bold; padding:7px 22px; margin:4px 2px; background:' + $theme_hex['accent'] + '; color:#ffffff; text-decoration:none; border-radius:3px;">动态</a>'
		*p '<a href="exec: gs ''themes'', ''menu_toggle'' & gt ''themes'', ''menu''" style="display:inline-block; font-size:1.15em; padding:7px 22px; margin:4px 2px; background:' + $tm_label_fg + '; color:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; text-decoration:none; border-radius:3px; opacity:0.5;">静态</a>'
	else
		*p '<a href="exec: gs ''themes'', ''menu_toggle'' & gt ''themes'', ''menu''" style="display:inline-block; font-size:1.15em; padding:7px 22px; margin:4px 2px; background:' + $tm_label_fg + '; color:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; text-decoration:none; border-radius:3px; opacity:0.5;">动态</a>'
		*p '<a href="exec: gs ''themes'', ''menu_toggle'' & gt ''themes'', ''menu''" style="display:inline-block; font-size:1.15em; font-weight:bold; padding:7px 22px; margin:4px 2px; background:' + $theme_hex['accent'] + '; color:#ffffff; text-decoration:none; border-radius:3px;">静态</a>'
	end
	*nl

	!! ── Theme table ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	!! Outer table: no background, just spacing between groups.
	!! Each group: plain label row + cells wrapped in a neutral-bg td (white/black)
	!! so the theme colour preview is always seen against a known background.
	!! The cells live in an inner table so they are never width-constrained.
	*p '<table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:0 6px;">'

	!! Classic ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	*p '<tr><td style="color:' + $tm_label_fg + '; padding:2px 4px;"><b>经典</b></td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $tm_cell_bg + '" style="background:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; padding:8px; border-radius:6px;"><table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:16px 0;"><tr>'
	if cfg_vars['themetype'] = 0:
		gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '默认', 'dynamic'
	else
		gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '白色', 'static'
		gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '黑色', 'static'
		gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '现代灰', 'static'
		gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '自定义', 'static'
	end
	*p '</tr></table></td></tr>'

	!! Catppuccin ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	*p '<tr><td style="color:' + $tm_label_fg + '; padding:2px 4px;"><b>Catppuccin</b></td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $tm_cell_bg + '" style="background:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; padding:8px; border-radius:6px;"><table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:16px 0;"><tr>'
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '拿铁',     $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '冰沙',    $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '玛奇朵', $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '摩卡',     $themes_menu_mode
	*p '</tr></table></td></tr>'

	!! Nord ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	*p '<tr><td style="color:' + $tm_label_fg + '; padding:2px 4px;"><b>Nord</b></td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $tm_cell_bg + '" style="background:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; padding:8px; border-radius:6px;"><table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:16px 0;"><tr>'
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '北欧浅色', $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '北欧深色',  $themes_menu_mode
	*p '</tr></table></td></tr>'

	!! Solarized ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	*p '<tr><td style="color:' + $tm_label_fg + '; padding:2px 4px;"><b>Solarized</b></td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $tm_cell_bg + '" style="background:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; padding:8px; border-radius:6px;"><table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:16px 0;"><tr>'
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '日光浅色', $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '日光深色',  $themes_menu_mode
	*p '</tr></table></td></tr>'

	!! Tokyo Night ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	*p '<tr><td style="color:' + $tm_label_fg + '; padding:2px 4px;"><b>Tokyo Night</b></td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $tm_cell_bg + '" style="background:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; padding:8px; border-radius:6px;"><table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:16px 0;"><tr>'
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '东京之夜浅色', $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '东京之夜',       $themes_menu_mode
	*p '</tr></table></td></tr>'

	!! Rosé Pine ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	*p '<tr><td style="color:' + $tm_label_fg + '; padding:2px 4px;"><b>Rosé Pine</b></td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $tm_cell_bg + '" style="background:' + $tm_cell_bg + '; padding:8px; border-radius:6px;"><table style="border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:16px 0;"><tr>'
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '玫瑰松·黎明', $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '玫瑰松',      $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'menu_cell', '玫瑰松·月夜', $themes_menu_mode
	*p '</tr></table></td></tr>'

	*p '</table>'
	*p '</center>'
	*nl

	!! ── Re-restore current theme after menu_cell has clobbered theme[*]/$theme_hex[*] ──
	$theme['name'] = $themes_menu_cur
	$theme['type'] = $themes_menu_mode
	gs 'themes', 'get_theme', 'indoors'
	bcolor = theme['bcolor']
	fcolor = theme['fcolor']
	lcolor = theme['lcolor']
	fsize  = theme['fsize']
	$fname = $theme['fname']
	$tm_fg_hex = func('shortgs', 'rgb_to_hex', fcolor)

	!! ── Lorem ipsum preview ───────────────────────────────────────────────
	'<hr>'
	'<b>预览</b>'
	*nl

	'凡事皆有其时，日复一日的辛劳终会换来片刻安宁。一个<font color="' + $theme_hex['bimbo'] + '">bimbo</font>走进了一家<font color="' + $theme_hex['goth'] + '">goth</font>咖啡馆，引发了一场<font color="' + $theme_hex['punk'] + '">punk</font>起义，空气中噼啪作响着<font color="' + $theme_hex['hypno'] + '">hypno</font>能量。 <a href="exec:gt ''themes'', ''menu''">路过的行人</a>早已见怪不怪，日子照旧一天天地过下去。人人都想分一杯羹，可真正得手的却寥寥无几。那些喧嚣终究只是过眼云烟，留下的只有挥之不去的疲惫。那个<font color="' + $theme_hex['bimbo'] + '">bimbo</font>平静地微笑着，那个<font color="' + $theme_hex['goth'] + '">goth</font>扬起眉毛，那个<font color="' + $theme_hex['punk'] + '">punk</font>调了调她的吉他。 <a href="exec:gt ''themes'', ''menu''">而那些装腔作势的人</a>终究只是在敷衍了事。那个<font color="' + $theme_hex['hypno'] + '">hypno</font>螺旋加深了，四个人都在随后的沉默中发现了意想不到的东西。'
	*nl

	!! ── Side-by-side: sample table (left) + progress bars (right) ───────
	*p '<table style="border-collapse:collapse; vertical-align:top;"><tr>'

	!! Left: alternating table_bg / table_bg_alt sample
	*p '<td valign="top" style="vertical-align:top; padding-right:32px;">'
	*p '<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse:collapse; min-width:320px;">'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第1行，A列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第1行，B列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + '; font-style:italic; opacity:0.7;">table_bg</td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第2行，A列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第2行，B列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + '; font-style:italic; opacity:0.7;">table_bg_alt</td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第3行，A列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第3行，B列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + '; font-style:italic; opacity:0.7;">table_bg</td></tr>'
	*p '<tr><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第4行，A列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + ';">第4行，B列</td><td bgcolor="' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '" style="background:' + $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'] + '; padding:6px 14px; color:' + $tm_fg_hex + '; font-style:italic; opacity:0.7;">table_bg_alt</td></tr>'
	*p '</table>'
	*p '</td>'

	!! Right: progress bars
	*p '<td valign="top" style="vertical-align:top;">'
	*p $func('progressbar', 'positive', 100, 0, 0, 0, '', '', '21,41,61,81')
	*p $func('progressbar', 'positive', 80, 0, 0, 0, '', '', '21,41,61,81')
	*p $func('progressbar', 'positive', 60, 0, 0, 0, '', '', '21,41,61,81')
	*p $func('progressbar', 'positive', 40, 0, 0, 0, '', '', '21,41,61,81')
	*p $func('progressbar', 'positive', 20, 0, 0, 0, '', '', '21,41,61,81')
	*p '</td>'

	*p '<td valign="top" style="vertical-align:top; padding-left:32px;">'
	*p $func('progressbar', 'mono:accent', 100)
	*p $func('progressbar', 'mono:accent', 80)
	*p $func('progressbar', 'mono:accent', 60)
	*p $func('progressbar', 'mono:accent', 40)
	*p $func('progressbar', 'mono:accent', 20)
	*p '</td>'

	*p '</tr></table>'

	killvar 'themes_menu_cur'
	killvar 'themes_menu_mode'
	killvar 'tm_label_fg'
	killvar 'tm_cell_bg'
	killvar 'tm_fg_hex'

	act '离开':
		$temp_ret_loc = $themes_menu_ret_loc
		$temp_ret_arg = $themes_menu_ret_arg
		settingmode = themes_menu_prev_settingmode
		themes_menu_active = 0
		killvar 'themes_menu_ret_loc'
		killvar 'themes_menu_ret_arg'
		killvar 'themes_menu_prev_settingmode'
		gt $temp_ret_loc, $temp_ret_arg
	end
	exit
end


	!!====================================================================!!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!                              HELPERS                               !!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!====================================================================!!


!! picks an alternating color between table_bg and table_bg_alt
!! use: $temp_bcolor = func('themes', 'alt_color', $temp_bcolor)

!! when you pass blank $temp_bcolor ('') it will return table_bg
!! when you pass $temp_bcolor again it will return table_bg_alt
if $ARGS[0] = 'alt_color':
	$result = iif($ARGS[1] = $theme_hex['table_bg'], $theme_hex['table_bg_alt'], $theme_hex['table_bg'])
	exit
end


!! wrapper for above func to use with wardrobe system
!! does the same thing but it sets $temp_bcolor instead of returning a value
!! use: gs 'themes', 'clothing'
if $ARGS[0] = 'clothing':
	$temp_bcolor = $func('themes', 'alt_color', $temp_bcolor)
	exit
end

!! DEPRECATED, remove in 0.10
if $ARGS[0] = 'wrap':
	$result = $func('wrap', $ARGS[1], $ARGS[2], $ARGS[3], $ARGS[4])
	exit
end

if $ARGS[0] = 'wrap_hex':
	$result = '<font color="' + $ARGS[1] + '">' + $ARGS[2] + '</font>'
	exit
end


if $ARGS[0] = 'wrap_thought':
	$result = $OpenInnerThought + $ARGS[1] + $CloseInnerThought
	exit
end




	!!====================================================================!!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!                              ROUTING                               !!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!====================================================================!!


if $ARGS[0] = 'outdoors':
	gs 'themes', 'apply', $ARGS[0]
	exit
end


if $ARGS[0] = 'indoors':
	gs 'themes', 'apply', $ARGS[0]
	gs 'indoors'
	exit
end


if $ARGS[0] = 'apply':
	if $theme['type'] = 'dynamic':
		theme['old_is_dark'] = theme['is_dark']

		gs 'themes', 'get_theme', $ARGS[1]

		if theme['old_is_dark'] <> theme['is_dark']:
			theme['old_is_dark'] = theme['is_dark']
			if $curloc <> '$menu_settings':
				clr
				$stat_msg = ''
				gs 'stat_display'
			end
		end
	else
		gs 'themes', 'get_theme', $ARGS[1]
	end
	bcolor = theme['bcolor']
	fcolor = theme['fcolor']
	lcolor = theme['lcolor']

	fsize = theme['fsize']
	$fname = $theme['fname']

	$noWillpower = $func('wrap', 'v_neg', '<br>你没有足够的意志力来执行这个动作。')
	$noMoney = $func('wrap', 'v_neg', '<br>你没有足够的钱来支付这个。')
	exit
end




	!!====================================================================!!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!                               APPLY                                !!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!====================================================================!!


if $ARGS[0] = 'reset':
	killvar 'theme'
	killvar 'theme_hex'

	killvar 'bcolor'
	killvar 'fcolor'
	killvar 'lcolor'

	fsize = 12
	$fname = 'Tahoma'

	theme['is_dark'] = 0
	exit
end


if $ARGS[0] = 'set_theme':
	gs 'themes', 'reset'
	$theme['name'] = $ARGS[1]
	$theme['type'] = $ARGS[2]
	theme['fsize'] = 12
	$theme['fname'] = 'Tahoma'
	exit
end


if $ARGS[0] = 'check_custom_vars':
	if $custom_theme['fname'] = '':		$custom_theme['fname']		= 'Tahoma'
	if custom_theme['fsize'] = 0:		custom_theme['fsize']		= 12
	if custom_theme['bcolor'] = 0:		custom_theme['bcolor']		= rgb(20, 20, 20)
	if custom_theme['fcolor'] = 0:		custom_theme['fcolor']		= rgb(255, 255, 255)
	if custom_theme['lcolor'] = 0:		custom_theme['lcolor']		= rgb(90, 180, 200)

	if $custom_theme['table_bg'] = '': $custom_theme['table_bg'] = '#202020'
	if $custom_theme['table_bg_alt'] = '': $custom_theme['table_bg_alt'] = '#303030'
	if $custom_theme['accent'] = '':	$custom_theme['accent']		= '#38C0D6'
	if $custom_theme['v_pos'] = '':		$custom_theme['v_pos']		= '#a6da95'
	if $custom_theme['pos'] = '':		$custom_theme['pos']		= '#eed49f'
	if $custom_theme['neutral'] = '':	$custom_theme['neutral']	= '#f5a97f'
	if $custom_theme['neg'] = '':		$custom_theme['neg']		= '#ee99a0'
	if $custom_theme['v_neg'] = '':		$custom_theme['v_neg']		= '#ed8796'
	if $custom_theme['punk'] = '':		$custom_theme['punk']		= '#c6a0f6'
	if $custom_theme['bimbo'] = '':		$custom_theme['bimbo']		= '#f5bde6'
	if $custom_theme['goth'] = '':		$custom_theme['goth']		= '#a5adcb'
	if $custom_theme['hypno'] = '':		$custom_theme['hypno']		= '#f5a97f'
	if $custom_theme['header'] = '':	$custom_theme['header']		= '#f0c6c6'
	exit
end




	!!====================================================================!!
	!!                                                                    !!
	!!                               THEMES                               !!

	!!====================================================================!!


if $ARGS[0] = 'default_theme':
	$theme['fname'] = 'Tahoma'
	theme['fsize'] = 12
	theme['is_dark'] = 0
	theme['bcolor']				= rgb(255, 255, 255)
	theme['fcolor']				= rgb(0, 0, 0)
	theme['lcolor']				= rgb(106, 90, 205)

	$theme_hex['table_bg']		= '#ffffff'
	$theme_hex['table_bg_alt']	= '#E6F2FF'
	!! Latte colors
	$theme_hex['accent']		= '#38C0D6' & !! custom accent color
	$theme_hex['v_pos']			= '#40a02b' & !! green
	$theme_hex['pos']			= '#df8e1d' & !! yellow
	$theme_hex['neutral']		= '#fe640b' & !! peach
	$theme_hex['neg']			= '#e64553' & !! maroon
	$theme_hex['v_neg']			= '#d20f39' & !! red
	$theme_hex['punk']			= '#8839ef' & !! mauve
	$theme_hex['bimbo']			= '#ea76cb' & !! pink
	$theme_hex['goth']			= '#6c6f85' & !! subtext0
	$theme_hex['hypno']			= '#fe640b' & !! peach
	$theme_hex['header']		= '#d24b4b' & !! Latte Flamingo darkened for light bg
	exit
end


if $ARGS[0] = 'get_theme':
	temp_theme_new_schema = 0
	gs 'themes', 'default_theme'

	if $theme['type'] = 'dynamic':
		if $theme['name'] = 'Dynamic 拿铁':
			theme['is_dark'] = 0
			if $ARGS[1] = 'indoors':
					theme['bcolor']              = rgb(239, 241, 245)
					theme['fcolor']              = rgb(76, 79, 105)
					theme['lcolor']              = rgb(30, 102, 245)

			theme['fcolor']				= rgb(224, 222, 244) & !! text
			theme['lcolor']				= rgb(156, 207, 216) & !! foam

			$theme_hex['table_bg']		= '#1f1d2e' & !! surface
			$theme_hex['table_bg_alt']	= '#26233a' & !! overlay
			$theme_hex['accent']		= '#31748f' & !! pine
			$theme_hex['v_pos']			= '#8dd3c7' & !! greener foam (custom)
			$theme_hex['pos']			= '#f6c177' & !! gold
			$theme_hex['neutral']		= '#ebbcba' & !! rose
			$theme_hex['neg']			= '#e45b83' & !! redder love (custom)
			$theme_hex['v_neg']			= '#c24b6a' & !! deep love (custom)
			$theme_hex['punk']			= '#c4a7e7' & !! iris
			$theme_hex['bimbo']			= '#eb6f92' & !! love
			$theme_hex['goth']			= '#6e6a86' & !! muted
			$theme_hex['hypno']			= '#f6c177' & !! gold
			$theme_hex['header']		= '#c6808c'
		elseif $theme['name'] = '玫瑰松·月夜':
			theme['is_dark'] = 1
			theme['bcolor']				= rgb( 35,	33,	54) & !! base
			theme['fcolor']				= rgb(224, 222, 244) & !! text
			theme['lcolor']				= rgb(156, 207, 216) & !! foam

			$theme_hex['table_bg']		= '#2a273f' & !! surface
			$theme_hex['table_bg_alt']	= '#393552' & !! overlay
			$theme_hex['accent']		= '#3e8fb0' & !! pine
			$theme_hex['v_pos']			= '#8fd2c6' & !! greener foam (custom)
			$theme_hex['pos']			= '#f6c177' & !! gold
			$theme_hex['neutral']		= '#ea9a97' & !! rose
			$theme_hex['neg']			= '#e45b83' & !! redder love (custom)
			$theme_hex['v_neg']			= '#c24b6a' & !! deep love (custom)
			$theme_hex['punk']			= '#c4a7e7' & !! iris
			$theme_hex['bimbo']			= '#eb6f92' & !! love
			$theme_hex['goth']			= '#6e6a86' & !! muted
			$theme_hex['hypno']			= '#f6c177' & !! gold
			$theme_hex['header']		= '#c6808c'
		elseif $theme['name'] = '玫瑰松·黎明':
			theme['is_dark'] = 0
			theme['bcolor']				= rgb(250, 244, 237) & !! base
			theme['fcolor']				= rgb( 87,	82,	121) & !! text
			theme['lcolor']				= rgb( 86, 148, 159) & !! foam

			$theme_hex['table_bg']		= '#fffaf3' & !! surface
			$theme_hex['table_bg_alt']	= '#f2e9e1' & !! overlay
			$theme_hex['accent']		= '#286983' & !! pine
			$theme_hex['v_pos']			= '#4d9a8e' & !! greener foam (custom)
			$theme_hex['pos']			= '#ea9d34' & !! gold
			$theme_hex['neutral']		= '#d7827e' & !! rose
			$theme_hex['neg']			= '#b0526f' & !! redder love (custom)
			$theme_hex['v_neg']			= '#8f3f56' & !! deep love (custom)
			$theme_hex['punk']			= '#907aa9' & !! iris
			$theme_hex['bimbo']			= '#dd7fa5' & !! pinker rose (custom)
			$theme_hex['goth']			= '#9893a5' & !! muted
			$theme_hex['hypno']			= '#ea9d34' & !! gold
			$theme_hex['header']		= '#9d5958'
		elseif $theme['name'] = '自定义':
			theme['is_dark'] = custom_theme['is_dark']
			theme['bcolor'] = custom_theme['bcolor']
			theme['fcolor'] = custom_theme['fcolor']
			theme['lcolor'] = custom_theme['lcolor']
			theme['fsize'] = custom_theme['fsize']
			$theme['fname'] = $custom_theme['fname']
			$theme_hex['table_bg']		= $custom_theme['table_bg']
			$theme_hex['table_bg_alt']	= $custom_theme['table_bg_alt']
			$theme_hex['accent']	= $custom_theme['accent']
			$theme_hex['v_pos']		= $custom_theme['v_pos']
			$theme_hex['pos']		= $custom_theme['pos']
			$theme_hex['neutral']	= $custom_theme['neutral']
			$theme_hex['neg']		= $custom_theme['neg']
			$theme_hex['v_neg']		= $custom_theme['v_neg']
			$theme_hex['punk']		= $custom_theme['punk']
			$theme_hex['bimbo']		= $custom_theme['bimbo']
			$theme_hex['goth']		= $custom_theme['goth']
			$theme_hex['hypno']		= $custom_theme['hypno']
			$theme_hex['header']	= $custom_theme['header']
		end
	else
		"对 themes / get_theme 的调用参数无效。你发现了错误！请告诉我们。"
		$theme['type']
	end
	exit
end
